### system prompt `formalize` (in effect; source: /home/axadmin/.config/metis/prompts/formalize.txt)

```text
You are a formalizer for LLP, a typed linear-logic behavior-catalog
language. Emit ONLY LLP source, no fences, no prose.
```

### system prompt `narrate` (in effect; source: /home/axadmin/.config/metis/prompts/narrate.txt)

```text
You are a storyteller. The user gives you the record of one run of a
story engine, and you retell it as a short prose story. You never
copy the record's format.

Truth. Tell only what the record says. Never add names, places,
feelings, motives, relations, deaths or outcomes it does not state.
Nobody dies, marries, leaves or kills unless an event line says so.
If it does not say why something happened, do not say why. Spell
every name exactly as the record does. The names may remind you of a
famous story — ignore that story completely; this record is a
different one, and only the record counts.

Meaning. Each event line reads "beat | rule [role=name,...] — what
happened". The text after the dash already has the names filled in:
retell THAT, in plain past tense, and keep who-does-what exactly as
written. After "⇒" comes what the event did, exactly: "gone" things ended
or were used up, "new" things began, "stays" things were only
involved and are still there. When the dash text is vague about who
did what, the ⇒ part decides: murdered(ada,bo) means ada murdered bo,
and anger(ada,bo) means ada is angry at bo — the first name is the
one who acts or feels. Never contradict the ⇒ part. Leave out engineering words:
tokens, atoms, consuming, "$-read", "read", premises, "sic". Lines like "act one settles -> courtship"
are scene changes: one short sentence, such as "Time passed."

Order. Retell the run beat by beat — never replace it with a summary
of themes. Tell the beats in number order. Never move an event to
another beat. Events in the SAME beat happen at the same time: join
them with "while" or "at the same time". Use "then" only between
beats. When one rule fires several times, say it once with a count
("twice", "three times") or a summary ("compliments went around"). In
a crowded run, give full sentences to the events that change the
most (deaths, marriages, departures, betrayals) and summarize the
rest briefly.

Ending. Close with one sentence from the final state, using only its
names: who or what remains. "(eve)" is one fact about eve, ":2" means
two of them, "·" means one.

Form. A short title of your own on the first line. Then the story,
one or two paragraphs, at most 220 words. Then one line beginning
"Narrator's note:" with one plain sentence naming the beat numbers
that held more than one event, and what you merged. No headings,
no lists, no markdown, nothing about the engine or the rules, and
never print the final state table. The narrator's note comes last.

EXAMPLE INPUT (its names never appear in your story):

T 1 — 6 events, 4 beats
  1 | light_lamp  [K=ida,T=north]  — ida lights the lamp in tower north.  ⇒ gone: oil; new: lit(north); stays: keeper(ida,north)
    | light_lamp  [K=ole,T=south]  — ole lights the lamp in tower south.  ⇒ gone: oil; new: lit(south); stays: keeper(ole,south)
  2 | ship_passes [T=north]  — a ship passes by its light.  ⇒ gone: ship; new: safe; stays: lit(north)
    | ship_passes [T=north]  — a ship passes by its light.  ⇒ gone: ship; new: safe; stays: lit(north)
  3 | dusk  — evening settles -> night.  ⇒ gone: evening; new: night
  4 | storm_out   [T=south]  — the storm strikes.  ⇒ gone: lit(south), storm; new: dark(south)
final state:
  dark    (south)
  lit     (north)
  safe    2

EXAMPLE OUTPUT:

The Two Lamps

Ida lit the lamp in the north tower while Ole lit the lamp in the
south tower. Then two ships passed the north tower by its light.
Night fell. Then the storm struck and put out the lamp in the south tower. In
the end the north lamp still burned, the south tower stood dark, and
two ships were safe.

Narrator's note: Beats 1 and 2 held two events each, and the two
ship passings were merged into "two ships".
```

heredoc:

```text
You are a formalizer for LLP, a typed linear-logic behavior-catalog
language. Emit ONLY LLP source: no fences, no prose, nothing else.

If the world is BLANK, emit ONE standalone base catalog (no `extends`)
otherwise emit a PACK over the base vocabulary you are shown (below).
```
```text
LLP syntax, exactly as shown (every declaration ends with "."; a
comment line starts with "%"; a doc line starts with "%%"):

catalog NAME 1.
provenance text "WHAT IT FORMALIZES" "YYYY-MM-DD".
layers (L ...).                       the rule layers
stages (S ...).                       stage names, each defined below
type T {c1 c2 c3}.                    a finite type (range: {n0..n4})
namespace NS produce (L ...).         which layers may produce its atoms
pred p(T1, T2) : NS.                  a RESOURCE predicate (linear state)
bwd q(T1, T2).                        a static (Horn) predicate ...
q(c1, c2).                            ... and its facts
stage S {
  %% a doc line quoting the approved text: MANDATORY before every rule
  name [L] : BODY -o HEAD.
}
%% a doc line (mandatory on links too)
qui name [L] : S1 -o S2.              switch stage when S1 can fire nothing more
#init ATOM N                          optional: an initial state (ground atoms)
#steps N

BODY is atoms joined by "*": a plain atom is CONSUMED, `$atom` is read
and KEPT, a bwd atom is a static guard, `X <> Y` requires distinct.
HEAD is atoms joined by "*", or `()` for nothing. `@w NUM` after the
head is the rule's relative choice weight among enabled rules
(default 1); alternatives share one body: `-o (h1 @w 0.7 | h2 @w 0.3)`.
Variables are Capitalized and take their types from the predicate
signatures; constants and all names are lowercase identifiers (letters,
digits, `_` or `-`; no dots). Every predicate used must be declared.

A complete, valid example (a toy, unrelated domain):

catalog bakery 1.
provenance text "a toy bakery" "2026-10-08".
layers (dyn).
stages (day night).
type oven {o1 o2}.
type grade {g0..g2}.
namespace bk produce (dyn).
pred dough : bk.
pred loaf(grade) : bk.
pred hot(oven) : bk.
pred cold(oven) : bk.
pred sold : bk.
bwd next(grade, grade).
next(g0, g1).
next(g1, g2).
stage day {
  %% "an oven heats up"
  heat [dyn] : cold(O) -o hot(O).
  %% "a hot oven bakes dough into a plain loaf, and stays hot"
  bake [dyn] : $hot(O) * dough -o loaf(g0) @w 3.
  %% "a loaf is improved one grade, or spoiled"
  finish [dyn] : loaf(G) * next(G, H) -o (loaf(H) @w 0.8 | () @w 0.2).
  %% "two ovens cannot be the same oven"
  swap [dyn] : hot(O) * cold(P) * O <> P -o cold(O) * hot(P).
  %% "a top-grade loaf is sold"
  sell [dyn] : loaf(g2) -o sold.
}
stage night {
  %% "at night a hot oven cools"
  cool [dyn] : hot(O) -o cold(O).
}
%% "when nothing more happens by day, night falls"
qui dusk [dyn] : day -o night.
#init dough 2
#init cold(o1) 1
#init cold(o2) 1
#steps 8

A PACK extends the base: it REUSES the base's types and predicates
(never re-declares them — a type is only ever declared with its
constants), may read and consume base atoms, declares its OWN new
predicates in its OWN namespace, and adds rules to the base's stages:

catalog PACKNAME 1.
extends BASENAME.
provenance text "WHAT IT FORMALIZES" "YYYY-MM-DD".
namespace NS2 produce (L ...).
pred newp(T1) : NS2.                  only NEW predicates/types here
stage S {                             a stage the base declares
  %% doc quoting the approved text
  name [L] : BODY -o HEAD.
}

A complete, valid pack over the bakery example above:

catalog bakery-delivery 1.
extends bakery.
provenance text "a toy pack" "2026-10-09".
namespace dl produce (dyn).
pred van(oven) : dl.
pred delivered(grade) : dl.
stage day {
  %% "a van at a hot oven takes a loaf away"
  load [dyn] : $van(O) * $hot(O) * loaf(G) -o delivered(G).
}
```

## 2026-10-09 10:59:44 — `mine Observed in the real elevators (instance 5; records of sinks/iter0-born.jsonl). The step state is written in these atoms: elevator_at_floor(E,F), elevator_closed(E), elevator_dir_up(E), person_waiting_up(F), person_waiting_down(F), person_in_elevator_going_up(E), person_in_elevator_going_down(E), with elevators e0 e1 and floors f0 f1 f2 f3; the world's own atoms (at, closed, open, heading, free, waiting, riding, delivered) are never observed. The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards. With close_door(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) appears (record 4). With open_door_going_up(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) disappears (record 9) and elevator_dir_up(e0) appears if absent (record 14); with open_door_going_down(e1) present, elevator_dir_up(e1) disappears (record 44). With move_current_dir(e0), elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present, elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) (records 5, 27), f1 becomes f2 (record 6), f2 becomes f3 (record 23); with move_current_dir(e0) and elevator_closed(e0) present but elevator_dir_up(e0) absent, elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) (record 113). With no action, person_waiting_up(f1), person_waiting_up(f2) and person_waiting_down(f2) appear at times (records 10, 26). With elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present and elevator_closed(e0) absent, person_waiting_up(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_up(e0) appears (record 109; likewise e1, record 8); with elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) present and both elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) absent, person_waiting_down(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_down(e0) appears (records 33, 68). No passenger was seen leaving an elevator in these 200 steps.`

```llp
pred open_door_going_down(elevator) : act.
pred move_current_dir(elevator) : act.
% hidden complements: linear logic cannot test absence, so each absent observed flag is carried by a complement atom
pred elevator_open(elevator) : hid.
pred elevator_dir_down(elevator) : hid.
pred slot_up(floor) : hid.
pred slot_down(floor) : hid.
% handshake: pol issues an action with pending, env reads the action and answers done, pol retires the action
pred ready(elevator) : hid.
pred pending(elevator) : hid.
pred done(elevator) : hid.
bwd above(floor, floor).
above(f0, f1).
above(f1, f2).
above(f2, f3).
bwd top(floor).
top(f3).
bwd bottom(floor).
bottom(f0).
stage run {
  %% "The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards."
  choose [pol] : ready(E) -o (close_door(E) * pending(E) @w 1 | open_door_going_up(E) * pending(E) @w 1 | open_door_going_down(E) * pending(E) @w 1 | move_current_dir(E) * pending(E) @w 2 | ready(E) @w 1).
  %% "The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards."
  retire_close [pol] : close_door(E) * done(E) -o ready(E).
  %% "The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards."
  retire_open_up [pol] : open_door_going_up(E) * done(E) -o ready(E).
  %% "The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards."
  retire_open_down [pol] : open_door_going_down(E) * done(E) -o ready(E).
  %% "The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards."
  retire_move [pol] : move_current_dir(E) * done(E) -o ready(E).
  %% "With close_door(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) appears (record 4)."
  close [env] : $close_door(E) * pending(E) * elevator_open(E) -o elevator_closed(E) * done(E).
  %% "With close_door(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) appears (record 4)."
  close_idle [env] : $close_door(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_closed(E) -o done(E).
  %% "With open_door_going_up(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) disappears (record 9)"
  open_up_keep [env] : $open_door_going_up(E) * pending(E) * elevator_closed(E) * $elevator_dir_up(E) -o elevator_open(E) * done(E).
  %% "With open_door_going_up(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) disappears (record 9) and elevator_dir_up(e0) appears if absent (record 14)"
  open_up_turn [env] : $open_door_going_up(E) * pending(E) * elevator_closed(E) * elevator_dir_down(E) -o elevator_open(E) * elevator_dir_up(E) * done(E).
  %% "With open_door_going_up(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) disappears (record 9)"
  open_up_again [env] : $open_door_going_up(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_open(E) * $elevator_dir_up(E) -o done(E).
  %% "elevator_dir_up(e0) appears if absent (record 14)"
  open_up_again_turn [env] : $open_door_going_up(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_open(E) * elevator_dir_down(E) -o elevator_dir_up(E) * done(E).
  %% "with open_door_going_down(e1) present, elevator_dir_up(e1) disappears (record 44)."
  open_down_turn [env] : $open_door_going_down(E) * pending(E) * elevator_closed(E) * elevator_dir_up(E) -o elevator_open(E) * elevator_dir_down(E) * done(E).
  %% "with open_door_going_down(e1) present, elevator_dir_up(e1) disappears (record 44)."
  open_down_keep [env] : $open_door_going_down(E) * pending(E) * elevator_closed(E) * $elevator_dir_down(E) -o elevator_open(E) * done(E).
  %% "with open_door_going_down(e1) present, elevator_dir_up(e1) disappears (record 44)."
  open_down_again_turn [env] : $open_door_going_down(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_open(E) * elevator_dir_up(E) -o elevator_dir_down(E) * done(E).
  %% "with open_door_going_down(e1) present, elevator_dir_up(e1) disappears (record 44)."
  open_down_again [env] : $open_door_going_down(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_open(E) * $elevator_dir_down(E) -o done(E).
  %% "With move_current_dir(e0), elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present, elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) (records 5, 27), f1 becomes f2 (record 6), f2 becomes f3 (record 23)"
  move_up [env] : $move_current_dir(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_closed(E) * $elevator_dir_up(E) * elevator_at_floor(E, F) * above(F, G) -o elevator_at_floor(E, G) * done(E).
  %% "with move_current_dir(e0) and elevator_closed(e0) present but elevator_dir_up(e0) absent, elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) (record 113)."
  move_down [env] : $move_current_dir(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_closed(E) * $elevator_dir_down(E) * elevator_at_floor(E, F) * above(G, F) -o elevator_at_floor(E, G) * done(E).
  %% "With move_current_dir(e0), elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present, elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f1)"
  move_open [env] : $move_current_dir(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_open(E) -o done(E).
  %% "f2 becomes f3 (record 23)"
  move_top [env] : $move_current_dir(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_closed(E) * $elevator_dir_up(E) * $elevator_at_floor(E, F) * top(F) -o done(E).
  %% "elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) (record 113)."
  move_bottom [env] : $move_current_dir(E) * pending(E) * $elevator_closed(E) * $elevator_dir_down(E) * $elevator_at_floor(E, F) * bottom(F) -o done(E).
  %% "With no action, person_waiting_up(f1), person_waiting_up(f2) and person_waiting_down(f2) appear at times (records 10, 26)."
  arrive_up [env] : slot_up(F) -o person_waiting_up(F) @w 0.2.
  %% "With no action, person_waiting_up(f1), person_waiting_up(f2) and person_waiting_down(f2) appear at times (records 10, 26)."
  arrive_down [env] : slot_down(F) -o person_waiting_down(F) @w 0.2.
  %% "With elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present and elevator_closed(e0) absent, person_waiting_up(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_up(e0) appears (record 109; likewise e1, record 8)"
  board_up [env] : $elevator_at_floor(E, F) * $elevator_dir_up(E) * $elevator_open(E) * person_waiting_up(F) -o person_in_elevator_going_up(E) * slot_up(F).
  %% "with elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) present and both elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) absent, person_waiting_down(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_down(e0) appears (records 33, 68)."
  board_down [env] : $elevator_at_floor(E, F) * $elevator_dir_down(E) * $elevator_open(E) * person_waiting_down(F) -o person_in_elevator_going_down(E) * slot_down(F).
}
#init ready(e0) 1
#init ready(e1) 1
#init elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) 1
#init elevator_at_floor(e1,f1) 1
#init elevator_open(e0) 1
#init elevator_open(e1) 1
#init elevator_dir_down(e0) 1
#init elevator_dir_up(e1) 1
#init slot_up(f0) 1
#init slot_up(f1) 1
#init slot_up(f2) 1
#init slot_down(f1) 1
#init slot_down(f2) 1
#init slot_down(f3) 1
#steps 200
```

## 2026-10-09 11:03:22 — `mine Observed interface (every record): a step starts from a state holding, for EACH observed fluent, either its atom (true) or not_<atom> (false) - e.g. elevator_closed(e0) or not_elevator_closed(e0), person_waiting_up(f1) or not_person_waiting_up(f1) - plus the action atoms taken that step (zero, one or several: close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E)) and one tick atom; no other atom is given. The next state is read when nothing more can happen, and must again hold, for each observed fluent, exactly one of the atom or not_<atom>. Several changes happen within ONE step: record 1 (actions move_current_dir(e1), open_door_going_up(e0)): elevator_at_floor(e1,f0) becomes elevator_at_floor(e1,f1), elevator_dir_up(e0) appears and person_waiting_down(f1) appears, all in that one step; record 37 (action close_door(e0)): elevator_closed(e0) appears, person_waiting_up(f1) becomes person_in_elevator_going_up(e0) and person_waiting_down(f2) appears, in one step; 17 of 200 steps change three or more atoms. In 74 of 200 steps nothing observed changes; 60 steps had no action and 22 of those still changed (arrivals, boarding). In 200 steps a waiting person appeared 30 times, only ever at f1 or f2 (person_waiting_up(f1) 8, person_waiting_down(f1) 8, person_waiting_up(f2) 5, person_waiting_down(f2) 9), never at f0 or f3. Observed in the real elevators (instance 5; records of sinks/iter0-born.jsonl). The step state is written in these atoms: elevator_at_floor(E,F), elevator_closed(E), elevator_dir_up(E), person_waiting_up(F), person_waiting_down(F), person_in_elevator_going_up(E), person_in_elevator_going_down(E), with elevators e0 e1 and floors f0 f1 f2 f3; the world's own atoms (at, closed, open, heading, free, waiting, riding, delivered) are never observed. The step state also carries action atoms close_door(E), open_door_going_up(E), open_door_going_down(E), move_current_dir(E), never observed afterwards. With close_door(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) appears (record 4). With open_door_going_up(e0) present, elevator_closed(e0) disappears (record 9) and elevator_dir_up(e0) appears if absent (record 14); with open_door_going_down(e1) present, elevator_dir_up(e1) disappears (record 44). With move_current_dir(e0), elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present, elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) (records 5, 27), f1 becomes f2 (record 6), f2 becomes f3 (record 23); with move_current_dir(e0) and elevator_closed(e0) present but elevator_dir_up(e0) absent, elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) becomes elevator_at_floor(e0,f0) (record 113). With no action, person_waiting_up(f1), person_waiting_up(f2) and person_waiting_down(f2) appear at times (records 10, 26). With elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) and elevator_dir_up(e0) present and elevator_closed(e0) absent, person_waiting_up(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_up(e0) appears (record 109; likewise e1, record 8); with elevator_at_floor(e0,f1) present and both elevator_closed(e0) and elevator_dir_up(e0) absent, person_waiting_down(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_down(e0) appears (records 33, 68). No passenger was seen leaving an elevator in these 200 steps.`

#### candidate snapshot (after `formalize`)

```llp
catalog elevators_riders 1.
extends elevators_observed.
provenance text "the observed real elevators (instance 5, sinks/iter0-born.jsonl): boarded passengers stay aboard; no passenger was seen leaving an elevator in 200 steps" "2026-10-09".
namespace rid produce (env).
pred watch_up(elevator) : rid.
pred watch_down(elevator) : rid.
pred aboard_up(elevator) : rid.
pred aboard_down(elevator) : rid.
stage run {
  %% "person_waiting_up(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_up(e0) appears (record 109; likewise e1, record 8) ... No passenger was seen leaving an elevator in these 200 steps."
  note_aboard_up [env] : watch_up(E) * $person_in_elevator_going_up(E) -o aboard_up(E).
  %% "person_waiting_down(f1) disappears and person_in_elevator_going_down(e0) appears (records 33, 68). No passenger was seen leaving an elevator in these 200 steps."
  note_aboard_down [env] : watch_down(E) * $person_in_elevator_going_down(E) -o aboard_down(E).
}
#init watch_up(e0) 1
#init watch_up(e1) 1
#init watch_down(e0) 1
#init watch_down(e1) 1
#steps 200
```
